#### We are building a web-based restaurant POS. Create the initial backend Entity Framework Core models and Minimal APIs for: 1. MenuItem (Name, Price, Category). 2. RestaurantTable (TableNumber, QRCodeString). 3. KitchenOrder (TableId, List of MenuItems, Status). Scaffold a local SQLite DbContext and write the POST endpoint to submit an order. Then, create a basic React frontend in a new 'ClientApp' folder with a menu interface that can call this endpoint.  

I'll help you build a restaurant POS system with Entity Framework Core models, Minimal APIs, and a React frontend. Let's break this down into steps:

1. First, I'll create the Entity Framework Core models for MenuItem, RestaurantTable, and KitchenOrder
2. Set up the DbContext with SQLite
3. Create the POST endpoint for submitting orders
4. Create the React frontend in a ClientApp folder

Let's start with the backend:

path/to/Models/MenuItem.cs
```
namespace RestaurantPOS.Models
{
    public class MenuItem
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public decimal Price { get; set; }
        public string Category { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
```

path/to/Models/RestaurantTable.cs
```
namespace RestaurantPOS.Models
{
    public class RestaurantTable
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public int TableNumber { get; set; }
        public string QRCodeString { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
```

path/to/Models/KitchenOrder.cs
```
namespace RestaurantPOS.Models
{
    public class KitchenOrder
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public int TableId { get; set; }
        public List<MenuItem> MenuItems { get; set; } = new List<MenuItem>();
        public string Status { get; set; } = "Pending";
    }
}
```

path/to/Data/AppDbContext.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RestaurantPOS.Models;

namespace RestaurantPOS.Data
{
    public class AppDbContext : DbContext
    {
        public AppDbContext(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext> options) : base(options) { }

        public DbSet<MenuItem> MenuItems { get; set; }
        public DbSet<RestaurantTable> RestaurantTables { get; set; }
        public DbSet<KitchenOrder> KitchenOrders { get; set; }

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            // Configure relationships
            modelBuilder.Entity<KitchenOrder>()
                .HasOne(ko => ko.Table)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(ko => ko.TableId);
        }
    }
}
```

path/to/Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RestaurantPOS.Data;
using RestaurantPOS.Models;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Add services to the container
builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseSqlite(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")));

builder.Services.AddCors();
builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();

var app = builder.Build();

// Configure the HTTP request pipeline
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI();
}

app.UseCors(policy => policy.AllowAnyOrigin().AllowAnyMethod().AllowAnyHeader());

app.UseHttpsRedirection();

// POST endpoint to submit an order
app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (KitchenOrder order, AppDbContext context) =>
{
    // Set default status if not provided
    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(order.Status))
    {
        order.Status = "Pending";
    }
    
    context.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
    await context.SaveChangesAsync();
    
    return Results.Created($"/api/orders/{order.Id}", order);
});

// GET endpoint to get all menu items
app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext context) =>
{
    return await context.MenuItems.ToListAsync();
});

// GET endpoint to get all tables
app.MapGet("/api/tables", async (AppDbContext context) =>
{
    return await context.RestaurantTables.ToListAsync();
});

app.Run();
```

path/to/ClientApp/src/App.js
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Menu from './Menu';

function App() {
  const [tables, setTables] = useState([]);
  const [selectedTable, setSelectedTable] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTables();
  }, []);

  const fetchTables = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('https://localhost:7001/api/tables');
      const data = await response.json();
      setTables(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching tables:', error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="App">
      <h1>Restaurant POS</h1>
      <div className="table-selection">
        <h2>Select a Table</h2>
        {tables.map(table => (
          <button 
            key={table.id} 
            onClick={() => setSelectedTable(table)}
            className={selectedTable?.id === table.id ? 'selected' : ''}
          >
            Table {table.tableNumber}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {selectedTable && <Menu table={selectedTable} />}
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

path/to/ClientApp/src/Menu.js
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

function Menu({ table }) {
  const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
  const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchMenuItems();
  }, []);

  const fetchMenuItems = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('https://localhost:7001/api/menuitems');
      const data = await response.json();
      setMenuItems(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
    }
  };

  const addToCart = (item) => {
    setCart([...cart, item]);
  };

  const removeFromCart = (index) => {
    const newCart = [...cart];
    newCart.splice(index, 1);
    setCart(newCart);
  };

  const submitOrder = async () => {
    if (cart.length === 0) return;

    const order = {
      tableId: table.id,
      menuItems: cart,
      status: "Pending"
    };

    try {
      const response = await fetch('https://localhost:7001/api/orders', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(order),
      });

      if (response.ok) {
        alert('Order submitted successfully!');
        setCart([]);
      } else {
        alert('Error submitting order');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error submitting order:', error);
    }
  };

  const categories = [...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

  return (
    <div className="menu">
      <h2>Menu for Table {table.tableNumber}</h2>
      <div className="menu-categories">
        {categories.map(category => (
          <div key={category} className="category-section">
            <h3>{category}</h3>
            <div className="menu-items">
              {menuItems.filter(item => item.category === category).map(item => (
                <div key={item.id} className="menu-item">
                  <span>{item.name}</span>
                  <span>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</span>
                  <button onClick={() => addToCart(item)}>Add</button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="cart">
        <h3>Cart</h3>
        {cart.map((item, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="cart-item">
            <span>{item.name}</span>
            <span>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</span>
            <button onClick={() => removeFromCart(index)}>Remove</button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="cart-total">
          <strong>Total: ₹{cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.price, 0).toFixed(2)}</strong>
        </div>
        <button onClick={submitOrder} disabled={cart.length === 0}>
          Submit Order
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Menu;
```

import React from 'react';
import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client';
import './index.css';
import App from './App';

const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root'));
root.render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <App />
  </React.StrictMode>
);

body {
  margin: 0;
  font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', 'Roboto', 'Oxygen',
    'Ubuntu', 'Cantarell', 'Fira Sans', 'Droid Sans', 'Helvetica Neue',

code {

}

.App {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 20px;
}

h1 {
  color: #333;
}

.table-selection {
  margin: 20px 0;
}

.table-selection button {
  margin: 5px;
  padding: 10px 20px;
  background-color: #4CAF50;
  color: white;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 5px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.table-selection button.selected {
  background-color: #45a049;
  border: 2px solid #4CAF50;
}

.menu {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  margin-top: 20px;
}

.menu-categories {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 800px;
}

.category-section {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  border: 1px solid #ddd;
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 10px;
}

.menu-items {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-top: 10px;
}

.menu-item {
  border: 1px solid #ddd;
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 10px;
  width: 200px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
}

.menu-item button {
  background-color: #2196F3;
  color: white;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 3px;
  padding: 5px 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.cart {
  margin-top: 20px;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 800px;
  border: 1px solid #ddd;
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 10px;
}

.cart-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 5px 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #eee;
}

.cart-item button {
  background-color: #f44336;
  color: white;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 3px;
  padding: 5px 10px;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.cart-total {
  margin: 10px 0;
  text-align: right;
}

.cart button {
  background-color: #4CAF50;
  color: white;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 5px;
  padding: 10px 20px;
  cursor: pointer;
  width: 100%;
}

.cart button:disabled {
  background-color: #cccccc;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}
```

This implementation includes:

1. Entity Framework Core models for MenuItem, RestaurantTable, and KitchenOrder
2. A DbContext with SQLite configuration
3. A POST endpoint for submitting orders
4. GET endpoints for menu items and tables
5. A React frontend with:
   - Table selection interface
   - Menu display organized by category
   - Cart functionality to add/remove items
   - Order submission to the backend

To run this application:
1. Set up the backend project with Entity Framework Core and SQLite
2. Run migrations to create the database
3. Start the backend server
4. In the ClientApp folder, run `npm install` and then `npm start`

The frontend will connect to the backend API endpoints to fetch menu items and tables, and submit orders.

First, you need to add the Entity Framework Core and SQLite packages to your project. You can do this by running the following commands in the Package Manager Console:

```sh

dotnet add package Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Sqlite
```

After adding these packages, you need to ensure that the necessary `using` directives are present in your files.

Here are the updated files with the necessary `using` directives:

### path/to/Data/AppDbContext.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RestaurantPOS.Models;

namespace RestaurantPOS.Data
{
    public class AppDbContext : DbContext
    {
        public AppDbContext(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext> options) : base(options) { }

        public DbSet<MenuItem> MenuItems { get; set; }
        public DbSet<RestaurantTable> RestaurantTables { get; set; }
        public DbSet<KitchenOrder> KitchenOrders { get; set; }

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            // Configure relationships
            modelBuilder.Entity<KitchenOrder>()
                .HasOne(ko => ko.Table)
                .WithMany()
                .HasForeignKey(ko => ko.TableId);
        }
    }
}
```

### path/to/Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RestaurantPOS.Data;
using RestaurantPOS.Models;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Add services to the container
builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseSqlite(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")));

builder.Services.AddCors();
builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();

var app = builder.Build();

// Configure the HTTP request pipeline
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI();
}

app.UseCors(policy => policy.AllowAnyOrigin().AllowAnyMethod().AllowAnyHeader());

app.UseHttpsRedirection();

// POST endpoint to submit an order
app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (KitchenOrder order, AppDbContext context) =>
{
    // Set default status if not provided
    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(order.Status))
    {
        order.Status = "Pending";
    }
    
    context.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
    await context.SaveChangesAsync();
    
    return Results.Created($"/api/orders/{order.Id}", order);
});

// GET endpoint to get all menu items
app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext context) =>
{
    return await context.MenuItems.ToListAsync();
});

// GET endpoint to get all tables
app.MapGet("/api/tables", async (AppDbContext context) =>
{
    return await context.RestaurantTables.ToListAsync();
});

app.Run();
```

To create a solution file for your project, you can use the `dotnet new sln` command to create a new solution file and then add your project to it using the `dotnet sln add` command.

Here are the steps to create a solution file and add your project to it:

1. Open a terminal or command prompt.
2. Navigate to the root directory of your project.
3. Run the following commands:

```sh
dotnet new sln -n RestaurantPOS
dotnet sln add path/to/RestaurantPOS.csproj
```

This will create a new solution file named `RestaurantPOS.sln` and add your project to it.

If you want to create a solution file and add your project to it using a script, you can use the following script:

### path/to/create_solution.sh
```
#!/bin/bash

# Navigate to the root directory of the project
cd path/to

# Create a new solution file
dotnet new sln -n RestaurantPOS

# Add the project to the solution
dotnet sln add RestaurantPOS.csproj
```

Make sure to replace `path/to` with the actual path to your project directory.

After running the script, you should have a `RestaurantPOS.sln` file in your project directory, and you can open the project in Visual Studio by double-clicking the solution file.

    {

        public string Category { get; set; } = string.Empty;

}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using RestaurantPOS.Data;
using RestaurantPOS.Models;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Add services to the container
builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseSqlite(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")));

builder.Services.AddCors();
builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen();

var app = builder.Build();

// Configure the HTTP request pipeline
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseSwagger();
    app.UseSwaggerUI();
}

app.UseCors(policy => policy.AllowAnyOrigin().AllowAnyMethod().AllowAnyHeader());

app.UseHttpsRedirection();

// POST endpoint to submit an order
app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (KitchenOrder order, AppDbContext context) =>
{
    // Set default status if not provided
    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(order.Status))
    {
        order.Status = "Pending";
    }
    
    context.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
    await context.SaveChangesAsync();
    
    return Results.Created($"/api/orders/{order.Id}", order);
});

// GET endpoint to get all menu items
app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext context) =>
{
    return await context.MenuItems.ToListAsync();
});

// GET endpoint to get all tables
app.MapGet("/api/tables", async (AppDbContext context) =>
{
    return await context.RestaurantTables.ToListAsync();
});

app.Run();
```

I have created the initial Entity Framework Core models and the `AppDbContext` for your POS system as requested. I have organized them into the `Models` and `Data` folders and configured the context to use SQLite.

Models/MenuItem.cs
```csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace POSSystem.Models
{
    public class MenuItem
    {
        [Key]
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public decimal Price { get; set; }
        public string Category { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
```

Models/RestaurantTable.cs
```csharp
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace POSSystem.Models
{
    public class RestaurantTable
    {
        [Key]
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string TableNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string QRCodeString { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
```

Models/KitchenOrder.cs
```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;

namespace POSSystem.Models
{
    public class KitchenOrder
    {
        [Key]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        public int TableId { get; set; }

        [ForeignKey("TableId")]
        public RestaurantTable? Table { get; set; }

        public List<MenuItem> MenuItems { get; set; } = new List<MenuItem>();

        public string Status { get; set; } = "Pending";
    }
}
```

Data/AppDbContext.cs
```csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Models;

namespace POSSystem.Data
{
    public class AppDbContext : DbContext
    {
        public AppDbContext(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext> options) : base(options)
        {
        }

        public AppDbContext() : base()
        {
        }

        public DbSet<MenuItem> MenuItems { get; set; }
        public DbSet<RestaurantTable> RestaurantTables { get; set; }
        public DbSet<KitchenOrder> KitchenOrders { get; set; }

        protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder optionsBuilder)
        {
            if (!optionsBuilder.IsConfigured)
            {
                optionsBuilder.UseSqlite("Data Source=pos_system.db");
            }
        }

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            // Configuring the many-to-many relationship between KitchenOrder and MenuItem
            modelBuilder.Entity<KitchenOrder>()
                .HasMany(o => o.MenuItems)
                .WithMany()
                .UsingEntity(j => j.ToTable(name: "KitchenOrderItems"));

            base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);
        }
    }
}
```

        import React, { useState } from 'react';

        const POSMenu = () => {
          const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

          const menuItems = [
            { Id: 1, Name: "Paneer Tikka", Price: 250.00, Category: "Starters" },
            { Id: 2, Name: "Butter Chicken", Price: 450.00, Category: "Main Course" },
            { Id: 3, Name: "Dal Makhani", Price: 300.00, Category: "Main Course" },
            { Id: 4, Name: "Gulab Jamun", Price: 120.00, Category: "Desserts" },
            { Id: 5, Name: "Veg Biryani", Price: 350.00, Category: "Main Course" },
            { Id: 6, Name: "Garlic Naan", Price: 60.00, Category: "Breads" },
          ];

          const addToCart = (item) => {
            setCart(prev => [...prev, item]);
          };

          const total = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.Price, 0);

          return (
            <div style={styles.container}>
              <div style={styles.mainContent}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                  <h1>POS Menu</h1>
                </header>
                <div style={styles.grid}>
                  {menuItems.map(item => (
                    <div key={item.Id} style={styles.card} onClick={() => addToCart(item)}>
                      <div style={styles.category}>{item.Category}</div>
                      <h2 style={styles.name}>{item.Name}</h2>
                      <p style={styles.price}>₹{item.Price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                  {cart.length === 0 ? <p>Cart is empty</p> : cart.map((item, index) => (
                    <div key={index} style={styles.cartItem}>
                      <span>{item.Name}</span>
                      <span>₹{item.Price.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <hr />
                <div style={styles.total}>
                  <strong>Total: ₹{total.toFixed(2)}</strong>
                </div>
                <button style={styles.checkoutBtn}>Place Order</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        };

        const styles = {
          container: {
            display: 'flex',
            height: '100vh',
            fontFamily: 'Segoe UI, Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif',
            backgroundColor: '#f4f7f6',
          },
          mainContent: {
            flex: 1,
            padding: '20px',
            overflowY: 'auto',
          },
          header: {
            marginBottom: '30px',
            borderBottom: '2px solid #ddd',
            paddingBottom: '10px',
          },
          grid: {
            display: 'grid',
            gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(200px, 1fr))',
            gap: '20px',
          },
          card: {
            backgroundColor: '#fff',
            padding: '20px',
            borderRadius: '12px',
            boxShadow: '0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            transition: 'transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s',
            textAlign: 'center',
            border: '1px solid #eee',
          },
          category: {
            fontSize: '0.8rem',
            color: '#888',
            textTransform: 'uppercase',
            marginBottom: '8px',

            fontSize: '1.2rem',

            fontWeight: 'bold',
            color: '#2c3e50',
          },
          sidebar: {
            width: '350px',
            backgroundColor: '#fff',
            borderLeft: '1px solid #ddd',
            padding: '20px',
            display: 'flex',
            flexDirection: 'column',

I have created the `POSMenu.jsx` component. It features a modern grid layout for menu items, a sidebar for the current order summary, and uses the properties defined in your `MenuItem.cs` model (`Id`, `Name`, `Price`, and `Category`).

I have used inline styles to ensure the "clean, modern" look is preserved without requiring external CSS files, and I've included a mock dataset to demonstrate the functionality.

src/components/POSMenu.jsx
```javascript
import React, { useState } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    // Mock data based on MenuItem.cs properties
    const [menuItems] = useState([
        { Id: 1, Name: "Paneer Tikka", Price: 250.00, Category: "Starters" },
        { Id: 2, Name: "Butter Chicken", Price: 450.00, Category: "Main Course" },
        { Id: 3, Name: "Dal Makhani", Price: 300.00, Category: "Main Course" },
        { Id: 4, Name: "Gulab Jamun", Price: 120.00, Category: "Desserts" },
        { Id: 5, Name: "Veg Biryani", Price: 350.00, Category: "Main Course" },
        { Id: 6, Name: "Garlic Naan", Price: 60.00, Category: "Breads" },
        { Id: 7, Name: "Chana Masala", Price: 280.00, Category: "Main Course" },
        { Id: 8, Name: "Mango Lassi", Price: 150.00, Category: "Beverages" },
    ]);

    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity (optional logic)
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.Id === item.Id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.Id === item.Id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.Id !== itemId));
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.Price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems.map((item) => (
                        <div 
                            key={item.Id} 
                            style={styles.card} 
                            onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                            onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                            onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                        >
                            <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.Category}</span>
                            <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.Name}</h2>
                            <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.Price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.Id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.Name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.Price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.Id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },
    mainSection: {
        flex: 1,
        padding: '40px',
        overflowY: 'auto',
    },
    header: {
        marginBottom: '30px',
    },
    title: {
        margin: 0,
        fontSize: '2.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    subtitle: {
        color: '#7f8c8d',
        fontSize: '1.1rem',
    },
    grid: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(220px, 1fr))',
        gap: '25px',
    },
    card: {
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        padding: '20px',
        borderRadius: '12px',
        border: '2px solid #e0e0e0',
        boxShadow: '0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
        textAlign: 'center',
    },
    categoryTag: {
        fontSize: '0.75rem',
        backgroundColor: '#ecf0f1',
        color: '#7f8c8d',
        padding: '4px 12px',
        borderRadius: '20px',
        textTransform: 'uppercase',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
    },
    itemName: {
        margin: '15px 0 10px 0',
        fontSize: '1.4rem',
        color: '#34495e',
    },
    itemPrice: {
        fontSize: '1.2rem',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
        color: '#27ae60',
    },
    sidebar: {
        width: '380px',
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        borderLeft: '1px solid #dee2e6',
        padding: '30px',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        boxShadow: '-5px 0 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)',
    },
    sidebarTitle: {
        margin: '0 0 20px 0',
        fontSize: '1.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    cartList: {
        flex: 1,
        overflowY: 'auto',
        marginBottom: '20px',

```csharp
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Add services to the container.
builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
{
    options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
        policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
               .AllowAnyMethod()
               .AllowAnyHeader()));

builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

var app = builder.Build();

// Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.MapOpenApi();
}

app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

app.UseHttpsRedirection();

app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext db) =>
    await db.MenuItems.ToListAsync());

app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
{
    var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
        new WeatherForecast
        (
            DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
            Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
            summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
        ))
        .ToArray();
    return forecast;
})
.WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

app.Run();

var summaries = new[]
{
    "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
};

record WeatherForecast(DateOnly Date, int TemperatureC, string? Summary)
{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}
```

```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5000/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity (optional logic)
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.Id === item.Id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.Id === item.Id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.Id !== itemId));
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.Price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems.map((item) => (
                        <div 
                            key={item.Id} 
                            style={styles.card} 
                            onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                            onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                            onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                        >
                            <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.Category}</span>
                            <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.Name}</h2>
                            <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.Price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.Id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.Name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.Price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.Id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },
    mainSection: {
        flex: 1,
        padding: '40px',
        overflowY: 'auto',
    },
    header: {
        marginBottom: '30px',
    },
    title: {
        margin: 0,
        fontSize: '2.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },

        color: '#7f8c8d',
        fontSize: '1.1rem',
    },
    grid: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(220px, 1fr))',
        gap: '25px',
    },

        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        padding: '20px',
        borderRadius: '12px',
        border: '2px solid #e0e0e0',
        boxShadow: '0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
        textAlign: 'center',
    },
    categoryTag: {
        fontSize: '0.75rem',
        backgroundColor: '#ecf0f1',
        color: '#7f8c8d',
        padding: '4px 12px',
        borderRadius: '20px',
        textTransform: 'uppercase',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
    },
    itemName: {
        margin: '15px 0 10px 0',
        fontSize: '1.4rem',
        color: '#34495e',
    },
    itemPrice: {
        fontSize: '1.2rem',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
        color: '#27ae60',
    },
    sidebar: {
        width: '380px',
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        borderLeft: '1px solid #dee2e6',
        padding: '30px',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        boxShadow: '-5px 0 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)',
    },
    sidebarTitle: {
        margin: '0 0 20px 0',
        fontSize: '1.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    cartList: {
        flex: 1,
        overflowY: 'auto',
        marginBottom: '20px',
    },
    emptyMsg: {
        color: '#bdc3c7',
        fontStyle: 'italic',
        textAlign: 'center',
        marginTop: '50px',
    },
    cartItem: {
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '12px 0',
        borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f1f1',
    },
    cartItemInfo: {
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
    },
    cartItemName: {
        fontWeight: '600',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    cartItemQty: {
        fontSize: '0.85rem',
        color: '#7f8c8d',
    },
    cartItemPrice: {
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: '10px',
    },
    removeBtn: {
        background: 'none',
        border: 'none',
        color: '#e74c3c',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        fontSize: '1.1rem',
    },
    footer: {
        borderTop: '2px solid #eee',
        paddingTop: '20px',
    },
    totalRow: {
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        fontSize: '1.5rem',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
        marginBottom: '20px',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    checkoutBtn: {
        width: '100%',
        padding: '15px',
        backgroundColor: '#27ae60',
        color: 'white',
        border: 'none',
        borderRadius: '8px',
        fontSize: '1.2rem',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'background 0.2s',
    },
};

export default POSMenu;
```

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

// Add services to the container.
builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

{
    options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
        policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
               .AllowAnyMethod()
               .AllowAnyHeader()));

builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

var app = builder.Build();

// Configure the HTTP request pipeline.

    app.MapOpenApi();
}

app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

app.UseHttpsRedirection();

var summaries = new[]
{
    "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
};

app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext db) =>
    await db.MenuItems.ToListAsync());

app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
{
    var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
        new WeatherForecast
        (
            DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
            Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
            summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
        ))
        .ToArray();
    return forecast;
})
.WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

app.Run();

record WeatherForecast(DateOnly Date, int TemperatureC, string? Summary)
{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}
```

}

```javascript
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);
...
```

- The JSON provided uses `id` (lowercase i).

- `return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];` -> `[...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }]`

- `<span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.Name}</span>` -> `item.name`
- `₹{(item.Price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}` -> `item.price`

```javascript
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity (optional logic)
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {

ClientApp\ClientApp\src\components\POSMenu.jsx
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity (optional logic)
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems.map((item) => (
                        <div 
                            key={item.id} 
                            style={styles.card} 
                            onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                            onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                            onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                        >
                            <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                            <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                            <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },

export default POSMenu;

app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (int[] itemIds, AppDbContext db) =>
{
    var order = new KitchenOrder
    {
        TableId = 1,
        Status = "Pending",
        MenuItems = new List<MenuItem>()
    };

    var items = await db.MenuItems
        .Where(m => itemIds.Contains(m.Id))
        .ToListAsync();

    order.MenuItems.AddRange(items);
    db.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
    await db.SaveChangesAsync();

    return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
});
```

    *   Fetch data from `http://localhost:5197/api/orders` using `useEffect`.
    *   Store the fetched data in a state.
    *   Render a dashboard with a grid of order cards.
    *   Each card must display:
        *   Order ID
        *   TableNumber (from the nested `table` object)
        *   Status
        *   A bulleted list of menu items (from the `menuItems` array).

        [
          {
            "id": 1,
            "tableId": 1,
            "table": {
              "id": 1,
              "tableNumber": "1",
              "qrCodeString": "table-1-qr"
            },
            "menuItems": [
              { "id": 1, "name": "Classic Burger", "price": 9.99, "category": "Mains" },
              { "id": 2, "name": "Fries", "price": 3.99, "category": "Sides" }
            ],
            "status": "Pending"
          }
        ]
        ```

    *   *Styling*: I'll use a similar style object to `POSMenu.jsx` for consistency.

    *   *Code Structure*:
        ```jsx
        import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

        const KitchenDisplay = () => {
            const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);

            useEffect(() => {
                fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/orders')
                    .then(res => res.json())
                    .then(data => setOrders(data))
                    .catch(err => console.error(err));
            }, []);

            return (
                <div style={styles.container}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>Kitchen Display</h1>
                    <div style={styles.grid}>
                        {orders.map(order => (
                            <div key={order.id} style={styles.card}>
                                <h3>Order #{order.id}</h3>
                                <p>Table: {order.table?.tableNumber}</p>
                                <p>Status: <strong>{order.status}</strong></p>
                                <ul>
                                    {order.menuItems.map(item => (
                                        <li key={item.id}>{item.name}</li>
                                    ))}
                                </ul>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>
            );
        };
        ```

    *   *Refining Styles*:
        - `container`: padding, background.
        - `grid`: display: grid, gap.
        - `card`: border, padding, shadow.

File 1: `ClientApp\ClientApp\src\components\KitchenDisplay.jsx`
```jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const KitchenDisplay = () => {
    const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/orders')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setOrders(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching orders:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            <header style={styles.header}>
                <h1 style={styles.title}>Kitchen Display</h1>
                <p style={styles.subtitle}>Active Orders</p>
            </header>

            <div style={styles.grid}>
                {orders.map((order) => (
                    <div key={order.id} style={styles.card}>
                        <div style={styles.cardHeader}>
                            <h2 style={styles.orderId}>Order #{order.id}</h2>
                            <span style={styles.statusTag}>{order.status}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p style={styles.tableInfo}>Table: {order.table?.tableNumber}</p>
                        <div style={styles.itemList}>
                            <h3 style={styles.listTitle}>Items:</h3>
                            <ul style={styles.bulletList}>
                                {order.menuItems.map((item, index) => (
                                    <li key={index}>{item.name}</li>
                                ))}
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                ))}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        padding: '40px',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        minHeight: '100vh',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },

    *   Endpoint: `/api/orders/{id}/complete`
    *   Method: `PUT`
    *   Parameters: `int id`, `AppDbContext db`
    *   Logic:
        1. Find `KitchenOrder` with the given `id`.
        2. If found:
            *   Set `Status` to "Completed".
            *   Call `db.SaveChangesAsync()`.
            *   Return `Results.Ok()`.
        3. If not found:
            *   Return `Results.NotFound()`.

    *   The user specified to add it below the `MapPost` endpoint for `/api/orders`.

    *   `Program.cs`
    *   `MapPost` block:
        ```csharp
        app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (List<int> itemIds, AppDbContext db) =>
        {
            var items = await db.MenuItems.Where(m => itemIds.Contains(m.Id)).ToListAsync();
            var order = new KitchenOrder 
            { 
                TableId = 1, 
                Status = "Pending", 
                MenuItems = items 
            };
            db.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
        });
        ```
    *   New block to insert:
        ```csharp
        app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, AppDbContext db) =>
        {
            var order = await db.KitchenOrders.FindAsync(id);
            if (order == null)
            {
                return Results.NotFound();
            }

            order.Status = "Completed";
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return Results.Ok();
        });
        ```

#### Update POSMenu.jsx to add a category filter. Extract the unique categories from the 'menuItems' state. Add a row of filter buttons above the menu grid that includes an "All" option alongside the unique categories. When a category is clicked, highlight the active button and filter the displayed menu items accordingly.  

To implement the category filter in `POSMenu.jsx`, we need to:

1. Extract unique categories from the `menuItems` state.
2. Add a row of filter buttons above the menu grid.
3. Highlight the active button when a category is clicked.
4. Filter the displayed menu items based on the selected category.

Here is the updated `POSMenu.jsx` file with the required changes:

ClientApp\ClientApp\src\components\POSMenu.jsx
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0) return;

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify(itemIds)
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
            } else {
                alert("Failed to place order.");
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                            className={activeCategory === category ? 'active' : ''}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },
    mainSection: {
        flex: 1,
        padding: '40px',
        overflowY: 'auto',
    },
    header: {
        marginBottom: '30px',
    },
    title: {
        margin: 0,
        fontSize: '2.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    subtitle: {
        color: '#7f8c8d',
        fontSize: '1.1rem',
    },
    categoryFilter: {
        display: 'flex',
        gap: '10px',
        marginBottom: '20px',
    },
    filterButton: {
        padding: '8px 16px',
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
        borderRadius: '4px',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
    },
    'filterButton.active': {
        backgroundColor: '#27ae60',
        color: '#ffffff',
        border: '1px solid #27ae60',
    },
    grid: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(220px, 1fr))',
        gap: '25px',
    },
    card: {
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',

To ensure that the category filter buttons render the 'category' text correctly and that the text color is dark enough to be visible against the button's background, we need to make a few adjustments to the `POSMenu.jsx` file.

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0) return;

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify(itemIds)
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
            } else {
                alert("Failed to place order.");
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                            className={activeCategory === category ? 'active' : ''}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },
    mainSection: {
        flex: 1,

        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart

            }

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

        setActiveCategory(category);

    return (

#### Update App.jsx to act as a simple router using state. 1) Add a state variable 'currentView' initialized to 'pos'. 2) At the top of the App, return a simple navigation bar (a div) with two buttons: "POS Menu" and "Kitchen Admin". 3) When "POS Menu" is clicked, set currentView to 'pos'. import './App.css';
import POSMenu from './components/POSMenu';
import KitchenDisplay from './components/KitchenDisplay';
import React, { useState } from 'react';

function App() {
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('pos');

  const handlePOSMenuClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('pos');
  };

  const handleKitchenAdminClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('admin');
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div style={styles.navBar}>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handlePOSMenuClick}>
          POS Menu
        </button>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handleKitchenAdminClick}>
          Kitchen Admin
        </button>
      </div>
      {currentView === 'pos' && <POSMenu />}
      {currentView === 'admin' && <KitchenDisplay />}
    </div>
  );
}

const styles = {
  navBar: {
    display: 'flex',

import './App.css';
import POSMenu from './components/POSMenu';
import KitchenDisplay from './components/KitchenDisplay';
import React, { useState } from 'react';

function App() {
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('pos');

  const handlePOSMenuClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('pos');
  };

  const handleKitchenAdminClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('admin');
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div style={styles.navBar}>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handlePOSMenuClick}>
          POS Menu
        </button>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handleKitchenAdminClick}>
          Kitchen Admin
        </button>
      </div>
      {currentView === 'pos' && <POSMenu />}
      {currentView === 'admin' && <KitchenDisplay />}
    </div>
  );
}

const styles = {
  navBar: {
    display: 'flex',
    justifyContent: 'space-around',
    padding: '10px',
    backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
    borderBottom: '1px solid #dee2e6',
  },
  navButton: {
    padding: '10px 20px',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
    borderRadius: '4px',
    cursor: 'pointer',
    transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
  },
};

export default App;

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0) return;

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify(itemIds)
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
            } else {
                alert("Failed to place order.");
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={activeCategory === category ? styles.activeCategoryBtn : styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={
