import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');
    const [selectedTable, setSelectedTable] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0 || selectedTable === '') {
            alert("Please select a table and add items to the cart.");
            return;
        }

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({ table: selectedTable, items: itemIds })
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
                setSelectedTable(''); // Reset selected table
            } else {
                alert("Failed to place order.");
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Table Selection */}
                <div style={styles.tableSelection}>
                    <label style={styles.label}>Select Table:</label>
                    <select 
                        value={selectedTable} 
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedTable(e.target.value)} 
                        style={styles.select}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select a table</option>
                        {Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map(tableNumber => (
                            <option key={tableNumber} value={tableNumber}>
                                Table {tableNumber}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={activeCategory === category ? styles.activeCategoryBtn : styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },
    mainSection: {
        flex: 1,
        padding: '40px',
        overflowY: 'auto',
    },
    header: {
        marginBottom: '30px',
    },
    title: {
        margin: 0,
        fontSize: '2.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    subtitle: {
        color: '#7f8c8d',
        fontSize: '1.1rem',
    },
    tableSelection: {
        marginBottom: '20px',
    },
    label: {
        marginRight: '10px',
        fontSize: '1rem',
        color: '#333',
    },
    select: {
        padding: '8px',
        fontSize: '1rem',
        border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
        borderRadius: '4px',
    },
    categoryFilter: {
        display: 'flex',
        gap: '10px',
        marginBottom: '20px',
    },
    filterButton: {
        padding: '8px 16px',
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
        borderRadius: '4px',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
        color: '#333', // Ensure text color is dark enough
    },
    activeCategoryBtn: {
        padding: '8px 16px',
        backgroundColor: '#27ae60',
        color: '#ffffff',
        border: '1px solid #27ae60',
        borderRadius: '4px',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
    },
    grid: {
        display: 'grid',
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(220px, 1fr))',
        gap: '25px',
    },
    card: {
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        padding: '20px',
        borderRadius: '12px',
        border: '2px solid #e0e0e0',
        boxShadow: '0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
        textAlign: 'center',
    },
    categoryTag: {
        fontSize: '0.75rem',
        backgroundColor: '#ecf0f1',
        color: '#7f8c8d',
        padding: '4px 12px',
        borderRadius: '20px',
        textTransform: 'uppercase',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
    },
    itemName: {
        margin: '15px 0 10px 0',
        fontSize: '1.4rem',
        color: '#34495e',
    },
    itemPrice: {
        fontSize: '1.2rem',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
        color: '#27ae60',
    },
    sidebar: {
        width: '380px',
        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
        borderLeft: '1px solid #dee2e6',
        padding: '30px',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        boxShadow: '-5px 0 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)',
    },
    sidebarTitle: {
        margin: '0 0 20px 0',
        fontSize: '1.5rem',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    cartList: {
        flex: 1,
        overflowY: 'auto',
        marginBottom: '20px',
    },
    emptyMsg: {
        color: '#bdc3c7',
        fontStyle: 'italic',
        textAlign: 'center',
        marginTop: '50px',
    },
    cartItem: {
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '12px 0',
        borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f1f1',
    },
    cartItemInfo: {
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
    },
    cartItemName: {
        fontWeight: '600',
        color: '#2c3e50',
    },
    cartItemQty: {
        fontSize: '0.85rem',
        color: '#7f8c8d',
    },
    cartItemPrice: {
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: '10px',
    },
    removeBtn: {
        background: 'none',
        border: 'none',
        color: '#e74c3c',
        cursor: 'pointer',
        fontSize: '1.1rem',
    },
    footer: {
        borderTop: '2px solid #eee',
        paddingTop: '20px',
    },
    totalRow: {
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',

    checkoutBtn: {

};

#### The text inside the top navigation buttons is invisible because it blends into the background. Update the inline style for both the "POS Menu" and "Kitchen Admin" buttons in App.jsx to explicitly set a dark text color (e.g., color: '#2c3e50', fontWeight: 'bold') so the labels are visible.

```
import './App.css';
import POSMenu from './components/POSMenu';
import KitchenDisplay from './components/KitchenDisplay';
import React, { useState } from 'react';

function App() {
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('pos');

  const handlePOSMenuClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('pos');
  };

  const handleKitchenAdminClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('admin');
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div style={styles.navBar}>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handlePOSMenuClick}>
          POS Menu
        </button>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handleKitchenAdminClick}>
          Kitchen Admin
        </button>
      </div>
      {currentView === 'pos' && <POSMenu />}
      {currentView === 'admin' && <KitchenDisplay />}
    </div>
  );
}

const styles = {
  navBar: {
    display: 'flex',
    justifyContent: 'space-around',
    padding: '10px',
    backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
    borderBottom: '1px solid #dee2e6',
  },
  navButton: {
    padding: '10px 20px',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
    borderRadius: '4px',
    cursor: 'pointer',
    transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
    color: '#2c3e50', // Set text color to dark
    fontWeight: 'bold', // Make text bold
  },
};

export default App;
```

To resolve the issue of the "Failed to place order" error, we need to ensure that the backend API endpoint `/api/orders` is correctly handling the POST request. Additionally, we should add some error handling and logging to the frontend to get more details about the error.

Here is the updated `POSMenu.jsx` file with improved error handling and logging:

ClientApp\ClientApp\src\components\POSMenu.jsx
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');
    const [selectedTable, setSelectedTable] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0 || selectedTable === '') {
            alert("Please select a table and add items to the cart.");
            return;
        }

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({ table: selectedTable, items: itemIds })
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
                setSelectedTable(''); // Reset selected table
            } else {
                const errorData = await response.json();
                console.error('Order placement failed:', errorData);
                alert(`Failed to place order: ${errorData.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Table Selection */}
                <div style={styles.tableSelection}>
                    <label style={styles.label}>Select Table:</label>
                    <select 
                        value={selectedTable} 
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedTable(e.target.value)} 
                        style={styles.select}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select a table</option>
                        {Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map(tableNumber => (
                            <option key={tableNumber} value={tableNumber}>
                                Table {tableNumber}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={activeCategory === category ? styles.activeCategoryBtn : styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },
    mainSection: {
        flex: 1,

    },

To implement logging using NLog in your .NET application, you need to follow these steps:

1. Install the NLog package.
2. Configure NLog in your application.
3. Add logging to your `Program.cs` file.

### Step 1: Install the NLog package

You can install the NLog package using the NuGet Package Manager Console:

```sh
Install-Package NLog.Web.AspNetCore
```

### Step 2: Configure NLog

Create a `nlog.config` file in the root of your project with the following content:

nlog.config
```
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8" ?>
<nlog xmlns="http://www.nlog-project.org/schemas/NLog.xsd"
      xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
      autoReload="true"
      throwConfigExceptions="true">

  <targets>
    <target xsi:type="File" name="logfile" fileName="logs/logfile.txt"
            layout="${longdate} ${uppercase:${level}} ${message} ${exception:format=tostring}" />
  </targets>

  <rules>
    <logger name="*" minlevel="Debug" writeTo="logfile" />
  </rules>
</nlog>
```

### Step 3: Add logging to `Program.cs`

Update your `Program.cs` file to include NLog configuration and add logging to the error handling section.

Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;
using NLog;
using NLog.Web;

var logger = NLogBuilder.ConfigureNLog("nlog.config").GetCurrentClassLogger();

try
{
    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

    // Add services to the container.
    builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
        options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

    builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
    {
        options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
            policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
                   .AllowAnyMethod()
                   .AllowAnyHeader());
    }); 

    builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

    var app = builder.Build();

    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        db.Database.EnsureCreated();

        // Seed Table 1 so the KitchenOrder has a valid foreign key
        if (!db.RestaurantTables.Any())
        {
            db.RestaurantTables.Add(new RestaurantTable { TableNumber = "1", QRCodeString = "table-1-qr" });
            db.SaveChanges();
        }

        if (!db.MenuItems.Any())
        {
            db.MenuItems.AddRange(
                new MenuItem { Name = "Classic Burger", Price = 9.99m, Category = "Mains" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Fries", Price = 3.99m, Category = "Sides" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Coke", Price = 1.99m, Category = "Beverages" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Caesar Salad", Price = 7.99m, Category = "Mains" }
            );
            db.SaveChanges();
        }
    }

    // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
    if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
    {
        app.MapOpenApi();
    }

    app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

    app.UseHttpsRedirection();

    var summaries = new[]
    {
        "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
    };

    app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.MenuItems.ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapGet("/api/orders", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.KitchenOrders
            .Include(o => o.MenuItems)
            .Include(o => o.Table)
            .ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (List<int> itemIds, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var items = await db.MenuItems.Where(m => itemIds.Contains(m.Id)).ToListAsync();
        var order = new KitchenOrder 
        { 
            TableId = 1, 
            Status = "Pending", 
            MenuItems = items 
        };
        db.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
    });

    app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var order = await db.KitchenOrders.FindAsync(id);
        if (order != null)
        {
            order.Status = "Completed";
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return Results.Ok();
        }
        return Results.NotFound();
    });

    app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
    {
        var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
            new WeatherForecast
            (
                DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
            ))
            .ToArray();
        return forecast;
    })
    .WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception exception)
{
    logger.Error(exception, "Stopped program because of exception");
    throw;
}
finally
{
    // Ensure to flush and stop internal timers/threads before application-exit (Avoid segmentation fault on Linux)
    LogManager.Shutdown();
}

record WeatherForecast(DateOnly Date, int TemperatureC, string? Summary)
{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}
```

### Step 4: Create the log folder

Create a `logs` folder in the root of your project to store the log files.

### Summary of Changes

1. **nlog.config**: Added NLog configuration to log errors to a file.
2. **Program.cs**: Integrated NLog for logging and added logging to the error handling section.
3. **logs folder**: Created a folder to store log files.

These changes will help you log errors and other important information to a file, making it easier to debug and monitor your application.

Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;
using NLog;
using NLog.Web;
using System.IO;

var logger = NLogBuilder.ConfigureNLog("nlog.config").GetCurrentClassLogger();

try
{
    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

    // Add services to the container.
    builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
        options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

    builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
    {
        options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
            policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
                   .AllowAnyMethod()
                   .AllowAnyHeader());
    }); 

    builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

    var app = builder.Build();

    // Ensure the logs directory exists
    var logDirectory = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), "logs");
    if (!Directory.Exists(logDirectory))
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(logDirectory);
    }

    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        db.Database.EnsureCreated();

        // Seed Table 1 so the KitchenOrder has a valid foreign key
        if (!db.RestaurantTables.Any())
        {
            db.RestaurantTables.Add(new RestaurantTable { TableNumber = "1", QRCodeString = "table-1-qr" });
            db.SaveChanges();
        }

        if (!db.MenuItems.Any())
        {
            db.MenuItems.AddRange(
                new MenuItem { Name = "Classic Burger", Price = 9.99m, Category = "Mains" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Fries", Price = 3.99m, Category = "Sides" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Coke", Price = 1.99m, Category = "Beverages" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Caesar Salad", Price = 7.99m, Category = "Mains" }

    // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.

        app.MapOpenApi();
    }

    app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

    app.UseHttpsRedirection();

    var summaries = new[]
    {
        "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
    };

    app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.MenuItems.ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapGet("/api/orders", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.KitchenOrders
            .Include(o => o.MenuItems)
            .Include(o => o.Table)
            .ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (List<int> itemIds, AppDbContext db) =>

        var order = new KitchenOrder

        await db.SaveChangesAsync();

####        ---> System.Text.Json.JsonException: The JSON value could not be converted to System.Collections.Generic.List`1[System.Int32]. Path: $ | LineNumber: 0 | BytePositionInLine: 1.  
####          at System.Text.Json.ThrowHelper.ThrowJsonException_DeserializeUnableToConvertValue(Type propertyType)

####          at System.Text.Json.Serialization.JsonConverter`1.TryRead(Utf8JsonReader& reader, Type typeToConvert, JsonSerializerOptions options, ReadStack& state, T& value, Boolean& isPopulatedValue)  
####          at System.Text.Json.Serialization.JsonConverter`1.ReadCore(Utf8JsonReader& reader, T& value, JsonSerializerOptions options, ReadStack& state)  
####          at System.Text.Json.Serialization.Metadata.JsonTypeInfo`1.ContinueDeserialize[TReadBufferState,TStream](TReadBufferState& bufferState, JsonReaderState& jsonReaderState, ReadStack& readStack, T& value)  
####          at System.Text.Json.Serialization.Metadata.JsonTypeInfo`1.DeserializeAsync[TReadBufferState,TStream](TStream utf8Json, TReadBufferState bufferState, CancellationToken cancellationToken)  
####          at System.Text.Json.Serialization.Metadata.JsonTypeInfo`1.DeserializeAsObjectAsync(PipeReader utf8Json, CancellationToken cancellationToken)

####          at Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.RequestDelegateFactory.<HandleRequestBodyAndCompileRequestDelegateForJson>g__TryReadBodyAsync|102_0(HttpContext httpContext, Type bodyType, String parameterTypeName, String parameterName, Boolean allowEmptyRequestBody, Boolean throwOnBadRequest, JsonTypeInfo jsonTypeInfo)  
####          --- End of inner exception stack trace ---

The error you're encountering is due to the JSON structure being sent from the frontend not matching the expected structure on the backend. The backend expects a list of integers (`List<int> itemIds`), but the frontend is sending an object with `table` and `items` properties.

To fix this, we need to update the backend to accept the correct JSON structure and also update the frontend to send the correct data.

### Backend Changes

Update the `Program.cs` file to handle the correct JSON structure:

Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;
using NLog;
using NLog.Web;
using System.IO;

var logger = NLogBuilder.ConfigureNLog("nlog.config").GetCurrentClassLogger();

try
{
    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

    // Add services to the container.
    builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
        options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

    builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
    {
        options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
            policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
                   .AllowAnyMethod()
                   .AllowAnyHeader());
    }); 

    builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

    var app = builder.Build();

    // Ensure the logs directory exists
    var logDirectory = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), "logs");
    if (!Directory.Exists(logDirectory))
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(logDirectory);
    }

    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        db.Database.EnsureCreated();

        // Seed Table 1 so the KitchenOrder has a valid foreign key
        if (!db.RestaurantTables.Any())
        {
            db.RestaurantTables.Add(new RestaurantTable { TableNumber = "1", QRCodeString = "table-1-qr" });
            db.SaveChanges();
        }

        if (!db.MenuItems.Any())
        {
            db.MenuItems.AddRange(
                new MenuItem { Name = "Classic Burger", Price = 9.99m, Category = "Mains" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Fries", Price = 3.99m, Category = "Sides" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Coke", Price = 1.99m, Category = "Beverages" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Caesar Salad", Price = 7.99m, Category = "Mains" }
            );
            db.SaveChanges();
        }
    }

    // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
    if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
    {
        app.MapOpenApi();
    }

    app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

    app.UseHttpsRedirection();

    var summaries = new[]
    {
        "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
    };

    app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.MenuItems.ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapGet("/api/orders", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.KitchenOrders
            .Include(o => o.MenuItems)
            .Include(o => o.Table)
            .ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (OrderRequest orderRequest, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var items = await db.MenuItems.Where(m => orderRequest.Items.Contains(m.Id)).ToListAsync();
        var order = new KitchenOrder 
        { 
            TableId = orderRequest.Table, 
            Status = "Pending", 
            MenuItems = items 
        };
        db.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
    });

    app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var order = await db.KitchenOrders.FindAsync(id);
        if (order != null)
        {
            order.Status = "Completed";
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return Results.Ok();
        }
        return Results.NotFound();
    });

    app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
    {
        var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
            new WeatherForecast
            (
                DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
            ))
            .ToArray();
        return forecast;
    })
    .WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception exception)
{
    logger.Error(exception, "Stopped program because of exception");
    throw;
}
finally
{
    // Ensure to flush and stop internal timers/threads before application-exit (Avoid segmentation fault on Linux)
    LogManager.Shutdown();
}

record WeatherForecast(DateOnly Date, int TemperatureC, string? Summary)
{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}

public class OrderRequest
{
    public int Table { get; set; }
    public List<int> Items { get; set; }
}
```

### Frontend Changes

Update the `POSMenu.jsx` file to send the correct JSON structure:

ClientApp\ClientApp\src\components\POSMenu.jsx
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');
    const [selectedTable, setSelectedTable] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0 || selectedTable === '') {
            alert("Please select a table and add items to the cart.");
            return;
        }

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({ table: parseInt(selectedTable), items: itemIds })
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
                setSelectedTable(''); // Reset selected table
            } else {
                const errorData = await response.json();
                console.error('Order placement failed:', errorData);
                alert(`Failed to place order: ${errorData.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Table Selection */}
                <div style={styles.tableSelection}>
                    <label style={styles.label}>Select Table:</label>
                    <select 
                        value={selectedTable} 
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedTable(e.target.value)} 
                        style={styles.select}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select a table</option>
                        {Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map(tableNumber => (
                            <option key={tableNumber} value={tableNumber}>
                                Table {tableNumber}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={activeCategory === category ? styles.activeCategoryBtn : styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {

        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',

### Summary of Changes

1. **Program.cs**: Updated the `MapPost` endpoint to accept an `OrderRequest` object instead of a list of integers.
2. **POSMenu.jsx**: Updated the `handlePlaceOrder` function to send the correct JSON structure with `table` and `items` properties.

These changes should resolve the error and allow the order to be placed successfully.

#### implement dependency injection call reposritory from service class rgiter them with serviers in program cs use interface and saprate make sure its loose copupling controller calls service and service call repository use diffrent folders for that  

To implement dependency injection, we will create interfaces for the repository and service, and then implement these interfaces in separate classes. We will register these services and repositories in `Program.cs`. Finally, we will update the controller to use the service.

Here are the changes:

1. Create a new folder `Repositories` and add a file `IMenuItemRepository.cs`.
2. Create a new folder `Services` and add a file `IMenuItemService.cs`.
3. Update `Program.cs` to register the repository and service.
4. Update `POSMenu.jsx` to use the service.

### File: Repositories\IMenuItemRepository.cs
```
using POSSystem.Models;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Repositories
{
    public interface IMenuItemRepository
    {
        Task<List<MenuItem>> GetAllMenuItemsAsync();
    }
}
```

### File: Repositories\MenuItemRepository.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;
using POSSystem.Repositories;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Repositories
{
    public class MenuItemRepository : IMenuItemRepository
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public MenuItemRepository(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<List<MenuItem>> GetAllMenuItemsAsync()
        {
            return await _context.MenuItems.ToListAsync();
        }
    }
}
```

### File: Services\IMenuItemService.cs

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Services
{
    public interface IMenuItemService
    {
        Task<List<MenuItem>> GetAllMenuItemsAsync();
    }
}

using POSSystem.Models;
using POSSystem.Repositories;
using POSSystem.Services;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Services
{
    public class MenuItemService : IMenuItemService
    {
        private readonly IMenuItemRepository _repository;

        public MenuItemService(IMenuItemRepository repository)
        {
            _repository = repository;
        }

        public async Task<List<MenuItem>> GetAllMenuItemsAsync()
        {
            return await _repository.GetAllMenuItemsAsync();
        }
    }
}
```

### File: Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Models;
using POSSystem.Repositories;
using POSSystem.Services;
using NLog;
using NLog.Web;
using System.IO;

var logger = NLogBuilder.ConfigureNLog("nlog.config").GetCurrentClassLogger();

try
{
    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

    // Add services to the container.
    builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
        options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

    builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
    {
        options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
            policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
                   .AllowAnyMethod()
                   .AllowAnyHeader());
    }); 

    builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

    // Register repositories
    builder.Services.AddScoped<IMenuItemRepository, MenuItemRepository>();

    // Register services
    builder.Services.AddScoped<IMenuItemService, MenuItemService>();

    var app = builder.Build();

    // Ensure the logs directory exists
    var logDirectory = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), "logs");
    if (!Directory.Exists(logDirectory))
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(logDirectory);
    }

    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        db.Database.EnsureCreated();

        // Seed Table 1 so the KitchenOrder has a valid foreign key
        if (!db.RestaurantTables.Any())
        {
            db.RestaurantTables.Add(new RestaurantTable { TableNumber = "1", QRCodeString = "table-1-qr" });
            db.SaveChanges();
        }

        if (!db.MenuItems.Any())
        {
            db.MenuItems.AddRange(
                new MenuItem { Name = "Classic Burger", Price = 9.99m, Category = "Mains" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Fries", Price = 3.99m, Category = "Sides" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Coke", Price = 1.99m, Category = "Beverages" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Caesar Salad", Price = 7.99m, Category = "Mains" }
            );
            db.SaveChanges();
        }
    }

    // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
    if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
    {
        app.MapOpenApi();
    }

    app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

    app.UseHttpsRedirection();

    var summaries = new[]
    {
        "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
    };

    app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (IMenuItemService service) =>
        await service.GetAllMenuItemsAsync());
	
    app.MapGet("/api/orders", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.KitchenOrders
            .Include(o => o.MenuItems)
            .Include(o => o.Table)
            .ToListAsync());
	
    app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (OrderRequest orderRequest, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var items = await db.MenuItems.Where(m => orderRequest.Items.Contains(m.Id)).ToListAsync();
        var order = new KitchenOrder 
        { 
            TableId = orderRequest.Table, 
            Status = "Pending", 
            MenuItems = items 
        };
        db.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
        await db.SaveChangesAsync();
        return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
    });

    app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var order = await db.KitchenOrders.FindAsync(id);
        if (order != null)
        {
            order.Status = "Completed";
            await db.SaveChangesAsync();
            return Results.Ok();
        }
        return Results.NotFound();
    });

    app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
    {
        var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
            new WeatherForecast
            (
                DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
            ))
            .ToArray();
        return forecast;
    })
    .WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception exception)
{
    logger.Error(exception, "Stopped program because of exception");
    throw;
}
finally
{
    // Ensure to flush and stop internal timers/threads before application-exit (Avoid segmentation fault on Linux)
    LogManager.Shutdown();
}

{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}

### File: ClientApp\ClientApp\src\components\POSMenu.jsx
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');
    const [selectedTable, setSelectedTable] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0 || selectedTable === '') {
            alert("Please select a table and add items to the cart.");
            return;
        }

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("http://localhost:5197/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({ table: parseInt(selectedTable), items: itemIds })
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
                setSelectedTable(''); // Reset selected table
            } else {
                const errorData = await response.json();
                console.error('Order placement failed:', errorData);
                alert(`Failed to place order: ${errorData.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Table Selection */}
                <div style={styles.tableSelection}>
                    <label style={styles.label}>Select Table:</label>
                    <select 
                        value={selectedTable} 
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedTable(e.target.value)} 
                        style={styles.select}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select a table</option>
                        {Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map(tableNumber => (
                            <option key={tableNumber} value={tableNumber}>
                                Table {tableNumber}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={activeCategory === category ? styles.activeCategoryBtn : styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },

using POSSystem.Models;
using POSSystem.Repositories;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Repositories
{

    }
}

import './App.css';
import POSMenu from './components/POSMenu';
import KitchenDisplay from './components/KitchenDisplay';
import OrderHistory from './components/OrderHistory';
import React, { useState } from 'react';

function App() {
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('pos');

  const handlePOSMenuClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('pos');
  };

  const handleKitchenAdminClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('admin');
  };

  const handleOrderHistoryClick = () => {
    setCurrentView('orderHistory');
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <div style={styles.navBar}>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handlePOSMenuClick}>
          POS Menu
        </button>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handleKitchenAdminClick}>
          Kitchen Admin
        </button>
        <button style={styles.navButton} onClick={handleOrderHistoryClick}>
          Order History
        </button>
      </div>
      {currentView === 'pos' && <POSMenu />}
      {currentView === 'admin' && <KitchenDisplay />}
      {currentView === 'orderHistory' && <OrderHistory />}
    </div>
  );
}

const styles = {
  navBar: {
    display: 'flex',
    justifyContent: 'space-around',
    padding: '10px',
    backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
    borderBottom: '1px solid #dee2e6',
  },
  navButton: {
    padding: '10px 20px',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    border: '1px solid #e0e0e0',
    borderRadius: '4px',
    cursor: 'pointer',
    transition: 'all 0.2s ease-in-out',
    color: '#2c3e50', // Set text color to dark
    fontWeight: 'bold', // Make text bold
  },
};

export default App;
```

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const OrderHistory = () => {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('http://localhost:5197/api/orders')
      .then(response => response.json())
      .then(data => {
        setOrders(data);
      })
      .catch(error => {
        console.error('Error fetching orders:', error);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1>Order History</h1>
      <ul>
        {orders.map(order => (
          <li key={order.id}>
            <strong>Order ID:</strong> {order.id}<br />
            <strong>Table:</strong> {order.tableNumber}<br />
            <strong>Items:</strong> {order.items.map(item => `${item.name} x${item.quantity}`).join(', ')}<br />
            <strong>Total:</strong> ${order.total.toFixed(2)}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
};

export default OrderHistory;
```

### Repositories/OrderRepository.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Interfaces;
using POSSystem.Models;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Repositories
{
    public class OrderRepository : IOrderRepository
    {
        private readonly AppDbContext _context;

        public OrderRepository(AppDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<List<KitchenOrder>> GetOrders()
        {
            return await _context.KitchenOrders
                .Include(o => o.MenuItems)
                .Include(o => o.Table)
                .ToListAsync();
        }

        public async Task CreateOrder(KitchenOrder order)
        {
            _context.KitchenOrders.Add(order);
            await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
        }

        public async Task CompleteOrder(int id)
        {
            var order = await _context.KitchenOrders.FindAsync(id);
            if (order != null)
            {
                order.Status = "Completed";
                await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Services/OrderService.cs
```
using POSSystem.Interfaces;
using POSSystem.Models;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace POSSystem.Services
{
    public class OrderService : IOrderService
    {
        private readonly IOrderRepository _orderRepository;

        public OrderService(IOrderRepository orderRepository)
        {
            _orderRepository = orderRepository;
        }

        public async Task<List<KitchenOrder>> GetOrders()
        {
            return await _orderRepository.GetOrders();
        }

        public async Task CreateOrder(KitchenOrder order)
        {
            await _orderRepository.CreateOrder(order);
        }

        public async Task CompleteOrder(int id)
        {
            await _orderRepository.CompleteOrder(id);
        }
    }
}
```

### Program.cs
```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Interfaces;
using POSSystem.Models;
using POSSystem.Repositories;
using POSSystem.Services;
using NLog;
using NLog.Web;
using System.IO;

var logger = NLogBuilder.ConfigureNLog("nlog.config").GetCurrentClassLogger();

try
{
    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

    // Add services to the container.
    builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
        options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

    builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
    {
        options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
            policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
                   .AllowAnyMethod()
                   .AllowAnyHeader());
    }); 

    builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

    builder.Services.AddScoped<IOrderRepository, OrderRepository>();
    builder.Services.AddScoped<IOrderService, OrderService>();

    var app = builder.Build();

    // Ensure the logs directory exists
    var logDirectory = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), "logs");
    if (!Directory.Exists(logDirectory))
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(logDirectory);
    }

    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        db.Database.EnsureCreated();

        // Seed Table 1 so the KitchenOrder has a valid foreign key
        if (!db.RestaurantTables.Any())
        {
            db.RestaurantTables.Add(new RestaurantTable { TableNumber = "1", QRCodeString = "table-1-qr" });
            db.SaveChanges();
        }

        if (!db.MenuItems.Any())
        {
            db.MenuItems.AddRange(
                new MenuItem { Name = "Classic Burger", Price = 9.99m, Category = "Mains" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Fries", Price = 3.99m, Category = "Sides" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Coke", Price = 1.99m, Category = "Beverages" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Caesar Salad", Price = 7.99m, Category = "Mains" }
            );
            db.SaveChanges();
        }
    }

    // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
    if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
    {
        app.MapOpenApi();
    }

    app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

    app.UseHttpsRedirection();

    var summaries = new[]
    {
        "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
    };

    app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (AppDbContext db) =>
        await db.MenuItems.ToListAsync());

    app.MapGet("/api/orders", async (IOrderService orderService) =>
        await orderService.GetOrders());

    app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (OrderRequest orderRequest, IOrderService orderService, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var items = await db.MenuItems.Where(m => orderRequest.Items.Contains(m.Id)).ToListAsync();
        var order = new KitchenOrder 
        { 
            TableId = orderRequest.Table, 
            Status = "Pending", 
            MenuItems = items 
        };
        await orderService.CreateOrder(order);
        return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
    });

    app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, IOrderService orderService) =>
    {
        await orderService.CompleteOrder(id);
        return Results.Ok();
    });

    app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
    {
        var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
            new WeatherForecast
            (
                DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
            ))
            .ToArray();
        return forecast;
    })
    .WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception exception)
{
    logger.Error(exception, "Stopped program because of exception");
    throw;
}
finally
{
    // Ensure to flush and stop internal timers/threads before application-exit (Avoid segmentation fault on Linux)
    LogManager.Shutdown();
}

record WeatherForecast(DateOnly Date, int TemperatureC, string? Summary)
{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}

public class OrderRequest
{
    public int Table { get; set; }
    public List<int> Items { get; set; }
}
```

        return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
    });

    app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, IOrderService orderService) =>
    {
        await orderService.CompleteOrder(id);
        return Results.Ok();
    });

    app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
    {
        var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
            new WeatherForecast
            (
                DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
            ))
            .ToArray();
        return forecast;
    })
    .WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception exception)
{
    logger.Error(exception, "Stopped program because of exception");
    throw;
}
finally
{
    // Ensure to flush and stop internal timers/threads before application-exit (Avoid segmentation fault on Linux)
    LogManager.Shutdown();
}

```
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using POSSystem.Data;
using POSSystem.Interfaces;
using POSSystem.Models;
using POSSystem.Repositories;
using POSSystem.Services;
using NLog;
using NLog.Web;
using System.IO;

var logger = NLogBuilder.ConfigureNLog("nlog.config").GetCurrentClassLogger();

try
{
    var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

    // Add services to the container.
    builder.Services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>
        options.UseSqlite("Data Source=restaurant.db"));

    builder.Services.AddCors(options =>
    {
        options.AddPolicy("AllowFrontend", policy =>
            policy.WithOrigins("http://localhost:5173")
                   .AllowAnyMethod()
                   .AllowAnyHeader());
    }); 

    builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

    builder.Services.AddScoped<IOrderRepository, OrderRepository>();
    builder.Services.AddScoped<IOrderService, OrderService>();
    builder.Services.AddScoped<IMenuItemRepository, MenuItemRepository>();
    builder.Services.AddScoped<IMenuItemService, MenuItemService>();

    var app = builder.Build();

    // Ensure the logs directory exists
    var logDirectory = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), "logs");
    if (!Directory.Exists(logDirectory))
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(logDirectory);
    }

    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        db.Database.EnsureCreated();

        // Seed Table 1 so the KitchenOrder has a valid foreign key
        if (!db.RestaurantTables.Any())
        {
            db.RestaurantTables.Add(new RestaurantTable { TableNumber = "1", QRCodeString = "table-1-qr" });
            db.SaveChanges();
        }

        if (!db.MenuItems.Any())
        {
            db.MenuItems.AddRange(
                new MenuItem { Name = "Classic Burger", Price = 9.99m, Category = "Mains" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Fries", Price = 3.99m, Category = "Sides" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Coke", Price = 1.99m, Category = "Beverages" },
                new MenuItem { Name = "Caesar Salad", Price = 7.99m, Category = "Mains" }
            );
            db.SaveChanges();
        }
    }

    // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
    if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
    {
        app.MapOpenApi();
    }

    app.UseCors("AllowFrontend");

    app.UseHttpsRedirection();

    var summaries = new[]
    {
        "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching"
    };

    app.MapGet("/api/menuitems", async (IMenuItemService menuItemService) =>
        await menuItemService.GetMenuItems());

    app.MapGet("/api/orders", async (IOrderService orderService) =>
        await orderService.GetOrders());

    app.MapPost("/api/orders", async (OrderRequest orderRequest, IOrderService orderService, AppDbContext db) =>
    {
        var items = await db.MenuItems.Where(m => orderRequest.Items.Contains(m.Id)).ToListAsync();
        var order = new KitchenOrder 
        { 
            TableId = orderRequest.Table, 
            Status = "Pending", 
            MenuItems = items 
        };
        await orderService.CreateOrder(order);
        return Results.Ok(new { message = "Order placed successfully" });
    });

    app.MapPut("/api/orders/{id}/complete", async (int id, IOrderService orderService) =>
    {
        await orderService.CompleteOrder(id);
        return Results.Ok();
    });

    app.MapGet("/weatherforecast", () =>
    {
        var forecast = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index =>
            new WeatherForecast
            (
                DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now.AddDays(index)),
                Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
                summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
            ))
            .ToArray();
        return forecast;
    })
    .WithName("GetWeatherForecast");

    app.Run();
}
catch (Exception exception)
{
    logger.Error(exception, "Stopped program because of exception");
    throw;
}
finally
{
    // Ensure to flush and stop internal timers/threads before application-exit (Avoid segmentation fault on Linux)
    LogManager.Shutdown();
}

record WeatherForecast(DateOnly Date, int TemperatureC, string? Summary)
{
    public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
}

public class OrderRequest
{
    public int Table { get; set; }
    public List<int> Items { get; set; }
}

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

const POSMenu = () => {
    const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);
    const [cart, setCart] = useState([]);
    const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('All');
    const [selectedTable, setSelectedTable] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        fetch('/api/menuitems')
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                setMenuItems(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error fetching menu items:', error);
            });
    }, []);

    const addToCart = (item) => {
        setCart((prevCart) => {
            // Check if item already exists in cart to increment quantity
            const existingItem = prevCart.find(i => i.id === item.id);
            if (existingItem) {
                return prevCart.map(i => i.id === item.id ? { ...i, quantity: (i.quantity || 1) + 1 } : i);
            }
            return [...prevCart, { ...item, quantity: 1 }];
        });
    };

    const removeFromCart = (itemId) => {
        setCart(prevCart => prevCart.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
    };

    const handlePlaceOrder = async () => {
        if (cart.length === 0 || selectedTable === '') {
            alert("Please select a table and add items to the cart.");
            return;
        }

        // Expand cart quantities into a flat array of IDs for the backend
        const itemIds = [];
        cart.forEach(item => {
            for (let i = 0; i < item.quantity; i++) {
                itemIds.push(item.id);
            }
        });

        try {
            const response = await fetch("/api/orders", {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({ table: parseInt(selectedTable), items: itemIds })
            });

            if (response.ok) {
                alert("Order placed successfully!");
                setCart([]); // Clear the cart
                setSelectedTable(''); // Reset selected table
            } else {
                const errorData = await response.json();
                console.error('Order placement failed:', errorData);
                alert(`Failed to place order: ${errorData.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(error);
            alert("Network error.");
        }
    };

    const totalAmount = cart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * (item.quantity || 1)), 0);

    const uniqueCategories = ['All', ...new Set(menuItems.map(item => item.category))];

    const handleCategoryFilter = (category) => {
        setActiveCategory(category);
    };

    return (
        <div style={styles.container}>
            {/* Main Menu Section */}
            <div style={styles.mainSection}>
                <header style={styles.header}>
                    <h1 style={styles.title}>POS Menu</h1>
                    <p style={styles.subtitle}>Select items to add to the order</p>
                </header>

                {/* Table Selection */}
                <div style={styles.tableSelection}>
                    <label style={styles.label}>Select Table:</label>
                    <select 
                        value={selectedTable} 
                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedTable(e.target.value)} 
                        style={styles.select}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select a table</option>
                        {Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map(tableNumber => (
                            <option key={tableNumber} value={tableNumber}>
                                Table {tableNumber}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>

                {/* Category Filter */}
                <div style={styles.categoryFilter}>
                    {uniqueCategories.map(category => (
                        <button 
                            key={category} 
                            style={activeCategory === category ? styles.activeCategoryBtn : styles.filterButton}
                            onClick={() => handleCategoryFilter(category)}
                        >
                            {category}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div style={styles.grid}>
                    {menuItems
                        .filter(item => activeCategory === 'All' || item.category === activeCategory)
                        .map((item) => (
                            <div 
                                key={item.id} 
                                style={styles.card} 
                                onClick={() => addToCart(item)}
                                onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#27ae60')}
                                onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0')}
                            >
                                <span style={styles.categoryTag}>{item.category}</span>
                                <h2 style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</h2>
                                <p style={styles.itemPrice}>₹{item.price.toFixed(2)}</p>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Order Summary Sidebar */}
            <div style={styles.sidebar}>
                <h2 style={styles.sidebarTitle}>Current Order</h2>
                <div style={styles.cartList}>
                    {cart.length === 0 ? (
                        <p style={styles.emptyMsg}>Your cart is empty</p>
                    ) : (
                        cart.map((item) => (
                            <div key={item.id} style={styles.cartItem}>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemInfo}>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemName}>{item.name}</span>
                                    <span style={styles.cartItemQty}>x{item.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style={styles.cartItemPrice}>
                                    ₹{(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
                                    <button 
                                        onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} 
                                        style={styles.removeBtn}
                                    >
                                        ✕
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))
                    )}
                </div>
                <div style={styles.footer}>
                    <div style={styles.totalRow}>
                        <span>Total:</span>
                        <span>₹{totalAmount.toFixed(2)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button style={styles.checkoutBtn} onClick={handlePlaceOrder}>Place Order</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

const styles = {
    container: {
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100vh',
        backgroundColor: '#f8f9fa',
        fontFamily: '"Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    },

        flex: 1,
        padding: '40px',

    },

    },
